\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sqrt{28}(2(M_{n}-1))^{3/2}}{\sqrt{\pi}(2M_{n}(M_{n}-1)\gamma^{2})}\sqrt{\frac{M_{n}(M_{n}-1)}{2}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{12}|^{4}]}

(601)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{M_{n}}{\gamma^{3}}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{12}|^{3}]+\sqrt{\frac{28}{\pi}}\sqrt{M_{n}\mathbb{E}[|Y_{12}|^{4}]},

(602)

which tends to zero if both (
35
) and (
36
) happen simultaneously.

V-M

Proof of Theorem
13

We first state two auxiliary lemmas.

Lemma 22

If
Z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, then for any random variable
T
, it holds that

\displaystyle d_{K}(T,Z)\leq 2(8\pi)^{-1/4}\sqrt{\tilde{d}_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)}.

(603)

Proof:

The proof is similar to the first part of the proof of Lemma
17
in Appendix
A-O
, so we omit this proof. ∎

Lemma 23

If
T
is a random variable such that
\mathbb{E}[T]=0
and
\var(T)=1
, and
Z
has the standard normal distribution, then

\displaystyle d_{K}(T,Z)<14(8\pi)^{-1/4}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[|T|]+\mathbb{E}\big[|T^{2}-1|\big]}.

(604)

Proof:

Appendix
A-Q
. ∎

Theorem
13
is a direct application of Lemma
23
by setting
T=g_{n}(P_{\mathrm{e}}(\mathcal{C}_{n}))
, gives a criterion for the convergence in distribution of the error exponent and any function of the error probability, in general.

Appendix A
Proofs of Lemmas

A-A

Proof of Lemma
1

Since
U_{n}\smash{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle({\rm d})}}{{\,\longrightarrow\,}}}U
, by Skorokhod’s representation theorem
[
23
]
, there exists a probability space
(\Omega,\mathcal{F},P)
and two random variables
V_{n}
and
V
such that
V_{n}\sim U_{n}
and
V\sim U
such that
V_{n}\xrightarrow{(\rm a.s.)}V
on
(\Omega,\mathcal{F},P)
. Now, for any fixed
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}[|V_{n}|^{1+\varepsilon}]

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\mathbb{E}_{P}[|V_{n}|^{2}]\bigg)^{(1+\varepsilon)/2}

(605)

\displaystyle=1<\infty,

(606)

where (
605
) follows from the concavity of the function
f(x)=x^{(1+\varepsilon)/2}
for any
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, and (
606
) follows from
\mathbb{E}_{P}[V_{n}^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[U_{n}^{2}]=1
. From (
606
), it follows that
V_{n}
is uniformly integrable on
(\Omega,\mathcal{F},P)

[
23
]
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[U]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}[V]

(607)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}[V_{n}]

(608)

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}[U_{n}]

(609)

\displaystyle=0.

(610)

On the other hand, for any fixed
n
, we have

\displaystyle|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|>\alpha\}\leq|V_{n}|^{2},

(611)

which satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{P}[|V_{n}|^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[|U_{n}|^{2}]=1.

(612)

Hence, by the dominated convergence theorem
[
30
]
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\alpha\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|>\alpha\}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[\lim_{\alpha\to\infty}|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|>\alpha\}\big]

(613)

\displaystyle=0

(614)

uniformly in
n
.

On the other hand, for each fixed
\alpha
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|>\alpha\}\big]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}[V_{n}^{2}]-\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|\leq\alpha\}\big]

(615)

\displaystyle=1-\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|\leq\alpha\}\big],

(616)

where (
616
) follows from (
612
). Note that

\displaystyle|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|\leq\alpha\}\leq\alpha^{2},

(617)

and

\displaystyle|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|\leq\alpha\}\xrightarrow{(\rm a.s.)}V^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V|\leq\alpha\}

(618)

by the continuous mapping theorem
[
23
]
and
V_{n}\xrightarrow{(\rm a.s.)}V
. Hence, by the dominated convergence theorem
[
23
]
, we also have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{N}|\leq\alpha\}\big]=\mathbb{E}_{P}[|V|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V|\leq\alpha\}]

(619)

point-wise in
\alpha
. Hence, from (
616
) and (
619
), we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|>\alpha\}\big]=1-\mathbb{E}_{P}[|V|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V|\leq\alpha\}]

(620)

point-wise in
\alpha
.

From (
614
) and (
620
), by Moore-Osgood theorem
[
31
]
on the interchange of limits, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{\alpha\to\infty}\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|\leq\alpha\}\big]

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\lim_{\alpha\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}\big[|V_{n}|^{2}\mathbf{1}\{|V_{n}|\leq\alpha\}\big]

(621)

\displaystyle=0,

(622)

which leads to
\{V_{n}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
be uniformly integrable. Hence, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{P}[V_{n}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{P}[V^{2}]

(623)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[U^{2}].

(624)

From (
612
) and (
624
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[U^{2}]=1.

(625)

A-B

Proof of Lemma
3

First, we prove that for any
\alpha>1
and
\lambda>0
, the following holds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{\alpha n}}\big]^{\frac{\alpha n}{\lambda}}\leq\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}\big]^{\frac{n}{\lambda}}.

(626)

Indeed, let

\displaystyle r=\frac{\lambda}{\alpha n},\qquad p=\frac{\lambda}{n},\qquad q=\frac{\lambda}{(\alpha-1)n}.

(627)

Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}=\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}

(628)

and
p,q,r\in(0,\infty)
if
\alpha>1
.
By applying the generalized Hölder’s inequality
[
30
,
3
]
, we have

\displaystyle\big(\mathbb{E}[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{r}]\big)^{\frac{1}{r}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\mathbb{E}[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{p}]\big)^{\frac{1}{p}}\big(\mathbb{E}[1^{q}]\big)^{\frac{1}{q}}

(629)

\displaystyle=\big(\mathbb{E}[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{p}]\big)^{\frac{1}{p}},

(630)

implying that (
626
) holds.

Since (
626
) holds for any
\alpha>1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}\big]^{\frac{n}{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\geq\lim_{\alpha\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{\alpha n}}\big]^{\frac{\alpha n}{\lambda}}

(631)

\displaystyle=2^{\mathbb{E}[\log P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)]}

(632)

where (
632
) follows from the identity
\mathbb{E}[\log X]=\lim_{x\to\infty}\log\mathbb{E}[X^{\frac{1}{x}}]^{x}
for any given
X>0
. From the definition of
E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)
in (
9
) and the definition of limit, we have that for every
\epsilon>0
there exists an
n_{0}
such that for
n>n_{0}
,

\displaystyle\Big|-\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}[\log P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)]-E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)\Big|<\epsilon.

(633)

Therefore, from (
632
) we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}\big]^{\frac{n}{\lambda}}\geq 2^{-n(1-\varepsilon)E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)}.

(634)

Thus, from (
634
) and (
626
), by letting
\varepsilon\to 0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\liminf_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}\big]\geq 2^{-\lambda E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)}.

(635)

Now, by the concavity of the function
x^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}
on
(0,\infty)
, we have by Jensen’s inequality that

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)\big]^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}

(636)

\displaystyle=2^{-\lambda E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)}.

(637)

where (
637
) follows from the fact that
\mathbb{E}[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)]\leq 2^{-nE_{\rm rce}(R,Q)}

[
3
,
28
]
.

From (
635
) and (
637
), under the condition that
E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)=E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n},Q)^{\frac{\lambda}{n}}\big]=2^{-\lambda E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)}.

(638)

This concludes the proof of this lemma.

A-C

Proof of Lemma
4

The upper bound follows from Bhattacharyya bound. Now, by
[
32
]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\big]\doteq 2^{-nE_{\rm rce}(R,Q)}

(639)

for all
R<R_{\rm{crit}}(Q)
. In addition, at this range of rate, the Bhattacharyya bound achieves the Gallager’s random coding bound
E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
. Hence, from (
639
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\big]\doteq 2^{-nE_{\rm rce}(R,Q)}

(640)

for all
R<R_{\rm{crit}}(Q)
, where
P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})
is the union bound on
P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n})
.

Now, for all rate
R<R_{\rm{crit}}(Q)
,
E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)=R_{0}(Q)-R
, where
R_{0}
is the cut-off rate corresponding to the underlying distribution
Q
, i.e.,

\displaystyle R_{0}(Q)=-\log\bigg(\sum_{y}\bigg(\sum_{x}Q(x)\sqrt{W(y|x)}\bigg)^{2}\bigg).

(641)

Let
Q_{X}=Q_{X}^{\prime}=Q
. By using standard KKT conditions for convex optimization, it is not hard to prove that

\displaystyle R_{0}(Q)=\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime}).

(642)

Hence, from (
642
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\big]

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{nR}2^{-n\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}

(643)

\displaystyle\doteq M_{n}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{XX^{\prime}})}2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})}.

(644)

Now, let
\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})
be the number of codeword pairs which have the same join type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
. Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})=\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\},

(645)

which leads to

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]

(646)

\displaystyle=M_{n}(M_{n}-1)2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{XX^{\prime}})}.

(647)

From (
644
) and (
647
), we have

\displaystyle(M_{n}-1)\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\big]\doteq\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})}.

(648)

On the other hand, observe that

\displaystyle(M_{n}-1)\mathbb{E}\big[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{P}\big(\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\big)\bigg].

(649)

From (
648
) and (
649
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{P}\big(\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\big)\bigg]=\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{XX^{\prime}})}2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})}.

(650)

Since (
650
) holds for all random i.i.d. codebook ensembles, hence for any fixed type
P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
, by choosing a sub-random codebook ensemble which contains all the codewords with the same joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\big|(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]\doteq 2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})},

(651)

or (
57
) holds.

A-D

Proof of Lemma
5

Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg[\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\geq 1\bigg]

(652)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]

(653)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\bigg]

(654)

\displaystyle=\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]

(655)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(656)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(657)

\displaystyle=M_{n}(M_{n}-1)\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(658)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}2^{2nR}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(659)

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2nR}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R}2^{-n(2R+\alpha(R))}

(660)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}2^{-n\alpha(R)}

(661)

for some
\alpha(R)>0
, where (
657
) follows from
[
29
]
, (
660
) follows from the fact that the number of possible joint types on
\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}
is sub-exponential in
n
.

A-E

Proof of Lemma
6

Define

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}}).

(662)

Then, we have

\displaystyle D_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(663)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(664)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\tilde{V}_{ij}.

(665)

It is easy to see that
\{\tilde{V}_{ij}\}_{i,j=1}^{M_{n}}
are pairwise independent. Hence, from (
665
), we have

\displaystyle\var(D_{n})=\frac{1}{M_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\var(\tilde{V}_{ij}).

(666)

Observe that

\displaystyle\var(\tilde{V}_{ij})=\var\bigg(\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)

(667)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\bigg(\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\bigg]\bigg)^{2}g_{n}^{2}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]

(668)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg(\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\bigg]\bigg)^{2}\bigg)g_{n}^{2}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(669)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg(\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\bigg)^{2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\bigg]\bigg)^{2}\bigg)g_{n}^{2}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(670)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]\bigg(1-\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]\bigg)g_{n}^{2}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(671)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2^{n\max_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}-\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}}),

(672)

where (
668
) follows from the pairwise independence of
\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\}_{i\neq j}
, and (
672
) follows from (
746
).

Hence, from (
666
) and (
672
), we obtain

\displaystyle\var(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\var(\tilde{V}_{ij})

\displaystyle\leq 2^{-2nR}\times 2^{n\max_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}-\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(673)

\displaystyle=2^{-2nR}\times 2^{n\max_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}-\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(674)

\displaystyle=2^{-2nR}\times 2^{n\max_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}-\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\bigg(\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)

(675)

\displaystyle=M_{n}2^{-2nR}\times 2^{-n\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\big(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]

(676)

\displaystyle=2^{-nR}\times 2^{-n\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R-\nu}\big(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}\mathbb{E}[D_{n}],

(677)

where (
677
) follows from the fact that the optimizer of the linear programming is in the boundary of the convex constraint set
[
33
]
.

On the other hand, from (
665
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{V}_{ij}]

(678)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(679)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(680)

\displaystyle=(M_{n}-1)\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(681)

\displaystyle\doteq(M-1)\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}

(682)

\displaystyle\doteq(M_{n}-1)2^{-n\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}

(683)

\displaystyle\doteq(M_{n}-1)2^{-n\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)},

(684)

where (
680
) follows from
[
29
]
, (
682
) follows from (
746
), and (
684
) follows from the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
is dense in
\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
.

Similarly, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}]\big]

(685)

\displaystyle=(M_{n}-1)\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(686)

\displaystyle=(M_{n}-1)\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}

(687)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-N\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})-R\big)}

(688)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})-R\big)},

(689)

where (
689
) follows from the fact
\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
is dense in
\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
.

It follows from (
689
) that

\displaystyle E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)+R=\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

(690)

Now, we have

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\geq 2R-\nu}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\geq 2R-\nu}2R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

(691)

\displaystyle\qquad=\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R-\nu}2R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

(692)

where (
692
) follows from the fact that the optimizer of the linear programming is on the boundary of the convex constraint set
[
33
]
.

Now, following the standard approach to solve the linear programming in (
692
) we have the following Lagrangian:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(P_{XX^{\prime}},\lambda)=2R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})+\lambda\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R+\nu\big).

(693)

By setting,

\displaystyle 0=\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(P_{XX^{\prime}},\lambda)}{\partial P_{XX^{\prime}}}(x,x^{\prime})=d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})+\lambda\bigg(\log\frac{P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}{Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}}+\log e\bigg),

(694)

we have

\displaystyle P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{e}Q_{X}(x)Q_{X^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})2^{-\frac{d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})}{\lambda}}.

(695)

Since

\displaystyle\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})=1,

(696)

we must choose
\lambda
such that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{e}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}Q_{X}(x)Q_{X^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})2^{-\frac{d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})}{\lambda}}=1.

(697)

Since
d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})=-\log\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\geq 0
, from (
697
) we must have

\lambda<0
, otherwise the LHS of (
697
) is less than or equal
1/e<1
.

With the choice
P_{XX^{\prime}}
in (
695
), we have

\displaystyle 2R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{X,X^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad=2R-\nu+\frac{1}{e}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})2^{-\frac{d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})}{\lambda}}

(698)

\displaystyle\qquad=2R-\nu-\frac{1}{e}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\log\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{\lambda}}.

(699)

Now, from (
697
), we have

\displaystyle 1

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{e}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}Q_{X}(x)Q_{X^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})2^{-\frac{d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})}{\lambda}}

(700)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{e}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}Q_{X}(x)Q_{X^{\prime}}(x^{\prime})\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{\lambda}}

(701)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{e}\bigg(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{\lambda}},

(702)

where (
702
) follows from the concavity of the function
x^{1/\lambda}
for
\lambda<0
and
x\in[0,1]
.

On the other hand, since
\lambda<0
, the function
-(\log x)x^{1/\lambda}
is convex in
x\in[0,1]
. Hence, by Jensen’s inequality, from (
699
), we have

\displaystyle 2R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{X,X^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad>2R-\nu-\frac{1}{e}\log\bigg(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{\lambda}}

(703)

\displaystyle\qquad=2R-\nu+\bigg[-\log\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\bigg(\sum_{x\in\mathcal{X}}Q(x)\sqrt{W(y|x)}\bigg)^{2}\bigg)\bigg]\bigg[\frac{1}{e}\bigg(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{\lambda}}\bigg]

(704)

\displaystyle\qquad=2R-\nu+R_{0}(Q)\bigg[\frac{1}{e}\bigg(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{\lambda}}\bigg]

(705)

\displaystyle\qquad\geq 2R-\nu+R_{0}(Q)

(706)

\displaystyle\qquad\geq R+E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)

(707)

for
\nu\leq 2R
, where in (
703
), the equality does not hold by the condition (
56
), (
705
) follows from
[
28
]
where
R_{0}(Q)
is the cut-off rate of the DMC under the underlying distribution
Q
, (
706
) follows from (
702
), and (
707
) follows from
[
28
]
.

From (
692
) and (
707
), we have

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\geq 2R-\nu}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R-\nu}2R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad>E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)+R

(708)

if
0\leq\nu\leq 2R
.

Therefore, from (
690
) and (
708
), we must have

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})<2R-\nu}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})=E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)+R.

(709)

It follows that

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad=\begin{cases}2R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}(x,x^{\prime}),&\qquad\mbox{if}\qquad D(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R-\nu,\\
R+E_{\rm rce}(R,Q),&\qquad\mbox{otherwise},\end{cases}

(710)

where (
710
) follows from (
709
). Here,
P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}
is an optimizer of
\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X^{\prime}})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})-R
.

From (
684
) and (
710
), we obtain (
63
).

Furthermore, from (
677
) and (
63
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\var(D_{n})}{\big(\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]\big)^{2}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}\begin{cases}2^{-n\nu},\qquad D(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R-\nu\\
2^{-n\big(\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R-\nu}R-\nu+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})-E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)\big)},\qquad\mbox{otherwise}\end{cases}

(711)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-\zeta(\nu,R)n}

(712)

for

\displaystyle\zeta(\nu,R)=\begin{cases}\nu,\qquad\mbox{if}\qquad D(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R-\nu\\
\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R}R+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})-E_{\rm rce}(R,Q),\qquad\mbox{otherwise}\end{cases}>0,

(713)

which is followed from (
708
) and
\nu>0
.

This concludes our proof of Lemma
6
.

A-F

Proof of Lemma
7

Recall the definition of
\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})
in Lemma
5
. For a pair of codewords with
(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big(\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\big)=g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}}).

(714)

\displaystyle P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{P}(\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j})

(715)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(716)

where (
716
) follows from (
714
).

First, we consider the case
R>0
. Take an arbitrary
\nu
such that
0<\nu\leq 2R
. Let

\displaystyle B_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}}),

(717)

\displaystyle\tilde{D}_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}}),

(718)

\displaystyle D_{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R-\nu}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}}).

(719)

Since
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]-\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]\to 0
as
\nu\to 0
and that
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]
and
\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]
are exponentially decaying in
n
, for
\nu
small enough, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]\leq\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]\leq\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]2^{\varepsilon n/2}.

(720)

Recall the typical set defined in Lemma
5
. For any given
\varepsilon>0
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigg|-\frac{\log P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})}{n}+\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\bigg|>\varepsilon\bigg]

(721)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigg|\log\frac{B_{n}}{\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]}\bigg|>\varepsilon n\bigg]

(722)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigg|\log\frac{B_{n}}{\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]}\bigg|>\varepsilon n\bigg|\mathcal{V}_{n}\bigg]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}]+\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c}]

(723)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigg|\log\frac{\tilde{D}_{n}}{\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]}\bigg|>\varepsilon n\bigg|\mathcal{V}_{n}\bigg]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}]+\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c}]

(724)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigg|\log\frac{\tilde{D}_{n}}{\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]}\bigg|>\varepsilon n\bigg]+\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c}]

(725)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\big[\tilde{D}_{n}>\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]2^{\varepsilon n}\big]+\mathbb{P}\big[\tilde{D}_{n}<\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]2^{-\varepsilon n}\big]+\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c}]

(726)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]}{2^{\varepsilon n}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]}+\mathbb{P}\big[D_{n}<\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]2^{-\varepsilon/2n}\big]+\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{N}^{c}]

(727)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{-\varepsilon n}+\mathbb{P}\big[D_{n}<\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]2^{-\varepsilon n}\big]+2^{-n\alpha(R)}

(728)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2^{-\varepsilon n}+\mathbb{P}\big[D_{n}<\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]2^{-(\varepsilon/2)n}\big]+2^{-n\alpha(R)}

(729)

where (
723
) follows from
\mathbb{P}(A)=\mathbb{P}(A|B)\mathbb{P}(B)+\mathbb{P}(A|B^{c})\mathbb{P}(B^{c})\leq\mathbb{P}(A|B)\mathbb{P}(B)+\mathbb{P}(B^{c})
, (
724
) follows from the fact that given
\mathcal{V}_{n}
, it holds that
B_{n}=D_{n}
, (
725
) follows from
\mathbb{P}(A|B)\mathbb{P}(B)\leq\mathbb{P}(A|B)\mathbb{P}(B)+\mathbb{P}(A|B^{c})\mathbb{P}(B^{c})=\mathbb{P}(A)
, (
727
) follows from Markov’s inequality, (
728
) follows from
D_{n}\leq\tilde{D}_{n}
and from Lemma
5
, (
729
) follows from
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{D}_{n}]\leq\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]2^{\varepsilon n/2}
for
\nu
sufficiently small by (
720
).

Now, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[D_{n}<\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]2^{-(\varepsilon/2)n}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\big[D_{n}-\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]<\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]\big(2^{-(\varepsilon/2)n}-1\big)\big]

(730)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\big[\big|D_{n}-\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]\big|>\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]\big(1-2^{-(\varepsilon/2)n}\big)\big]

(731)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}\frac{\var(D_{n})}{\big(\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]\big)^{2}}

(732)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}2^{-n\beta(\nu,R)},

(733)

where (
733
) follows from Lemma
6
.

From (
729
) and (
733
), for any
\varepsilon>0
and
R>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigg|-\frac{\log P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})}{n}+\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\bigg|>\varepsilon\bigg]

(734)

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}2^{-\varepsilon n}+2^{-n\beta(\nu,R)}+2^{-n\alpha(R)}.

(735)

It follows from (
735
) that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigg|-\frac{\log P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})}{n}+\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)<\infty.

(736)

Hence, by Borel-Cantelli’s lemma
[
23
]
, we have

\displaystyle-\frac{\log P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})}{n}+\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\xrightarrow{(\rm a.s.)}0

(737)

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\bigg|-\frac{\log P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n}))}{n}+\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq-\frac{\log P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})}{n}+\bigg|-\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\bigg|

(738)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq-\frac{1}{n}\log P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n})+\bigg|-\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\bigg|

(739)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq E_{\rm{sp}}(R)+\bigg|-\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\bigg|,

(740)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq E_{\rm{sp}}(R)+\bigg|-\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{\mathbb{E}[D_{n}]}{M_{n}}\bigg)\bigg|,

(741)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq E_{\rm{sp}}(R)+R+\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

(742)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq E_{\rm{sp}}(R)+R+D_{\mathrm{b}}<\infty,

(743)

where (
740
) follows from
[
28
]
, (
741
), and (
742
) follows from Lemma
6
, where (
743
) follows with the fact that
d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})\leq D_{\mathrm{b}}<\infty
for all
x,x^{\prime}
by the condition (
56
).

From (
737
), (
743
), and the bounded convergence theorem
[
23
]
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}\bigg[-\frac{\log P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})}{n}+\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)\bigg]=0.

(744)

Now, by Lemma
6
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}-\frac{1}{n}\log\bigg(\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad=\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})-R\big).

(745)

Hence, we obtain (
66
) from (
744
) and (
745
). Note that (
67
) can be achieved from (
66
) by using (
710
) with
\nu=0
.

A-G

Proof of Lemma
8

Let
g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})
be the pairwise error probability given the joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
for
P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
. By Lemma
4
, the pairwise error probability can be expressed as

\displaystyle g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\doteq 2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})},

(746)

where

\displaystyle d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})=-\log\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg).

(747)

Now, let

\displaystyle\tilde{V}_{ij}=\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}}).

(748)

Now, let

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})g_{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(749)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}g_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})

(750)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\tilde{V}_{ij},

(751)

\displaystyle A_{n}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\tilde{P}_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})].

(752)

From Lemma
6
and Lemma
7
, we have

\displaystyle A_{n}=2^{-nE_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)}.

(753)

Now, recall the definition of the typical set
\mathcal{V}_{n}
in Lemma
5
. Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})>\frac{1}{2}2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}\bigg]+\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})<2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}>\frac{1}{2}2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}-A_{n}]\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}<2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}-A_{n}\bigg]

(754)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>\frac{1}{2}2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}-A_{n}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>A_{n}-2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}\bigg]

(755)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>\frac{1}{2}2^{-N(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}-A_{n}|\mathcal{V}_{n}]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}]+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>A_{n}-2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}|\mathcal{V}_{n}\bigg]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}]+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c})

(756)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|\tilde{P}_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>\frac{1}{2}2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}-A_{n}|\mathcal{V}_{n}]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}]+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|\tilde{P}_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>A_{n}-2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}|\mathcal{V}_{n}\bigg]\mathbb{P}[\mathcal{V}_{n}]+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c})

(757)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|\tilde{P}_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>\frac{1}{2}2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}-A_{n}]+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbb{P}\bigg[\big|\tilde{P}_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})-A_{n}\big|>A_{n}-2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}\bigg]+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{V}_{n}^{c})

(758)

\displaystyle\qquad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}\frac{\var(\tilde{P}_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n}))}{2^{-2nE_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)}}+2^{-\alpha(R)n}

(759)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{2nE_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)}\frac{1}{M_{n}^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\var(\tilde{V}_{ij})+2^{-\alpha(R)n}

(760)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{2nE_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)}2^{-2nR}2^{n\max_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}-\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}2^{nR}2^{-nE_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)}+2^{-\alpha(R)N}

(761)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{-n(R-E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})}+2^{-\alpha(R)n},

(762)

where (
756
) follows from
\mathbb{P}(A)=\mathbb{P}(A|B)\mathbb{P}(B)+\mathbb{P}(A|B^{c})\mathbb{P}(B^{c})\leq\mathbb{P}(A|B)\mathbb{P}(B)+\mathbb{P}(B^{c})
, (
759
) follows from Chebyshev’s inequality, (
684
), and Lemma
5
, (
760
) follows from the pairwise independence of
\tilde{V}_{ij}
, and (
761
) follows from (
677
) and
M_{n}\doteq 2^{nR}
.

Now, for the case
E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)=E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
, we must have

\displaystyle R+\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R}R+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

(763)

\displaystyle\geq\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\geq 2R}R+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

(764)

\displaystyle>E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)

(765)

\displaystyle=E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q),

(766)

where (
765
) follows from (
708
) by setting
\nu=0
.

Hence, for this case, from (
762
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})>\frac{1}{2}2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}\bigg]+\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})<2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}\bigg]\leq 2^{-n\Delta}

(767)

for some
\Delta>0
.

Now, we consider the case
E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)>E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
. First of all we need the following lemma, which extends
[
7
, Th. 2.1]
to the
DMC
case.

Lemma 24

In a DMC, for all rates for which
E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)=E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)+\Delta E
,
\Delta E>0
the probability that
a code of length
n
and rate
R
from the RCE has a codeword pair with empirical joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
such that

D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})>2R
goes to
0
exponentially fast as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Furthermore, if
P_{XX^{\prime}}
is such that
D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R
, the probability that the number of codeword pairs with joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
satisfies
\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\doteq 2^{-n(2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}))}
goes to one exponentially fast as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

Proof:

Let
\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})
be the number of codeword pairs with joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\geq 1\big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\big]

(768)

\displaystyle=\frac{M(M-1)}{2}\text{Pr}\left[P_{XX^{\prime}}\right]

(769)

\displaystyle\doteq e^{n2R}e^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(770)

\displaystyle=2^{-n\left(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R\right)}

(771)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R\big)}\to 0,

(772)

where (
768
) follows from the Markov’s inequality,
\mathbb{P}\{P_{XX^{\prime}}\}
in (
769
) indicates the probability to find a codeword pair with joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
and (
770
) follows from
[
19
, Th. 11.1.4]

Similarly, it is not hard to see that

\displaystyle\var(\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}))\doteq 2^{-n\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R\big)}.

(773)

The second statement can be proven by observing that by Chebyshev’s inequality and (
773
), for any positive number
\Delta
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\left|\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})-E\left[{\mathcal{N}}_{n}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\right]\right|>2^{-n(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R+\Delta)}\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}2^{-n(2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2\Delta)}.

(774)

Furthermore, since
\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]\doteq 2^{-n(2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}))}
, from (
774
) the probability that the number of codeword pairs with joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
satisfies
\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\doteq 2^{-n(2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}))}
goes to one exponentially fast as
n\rightarrow\infty
. As a closing remark, note that the region defined by the inequality
D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R
is the equivalent for general
DMC
to the Gilbert-Varshamov region in
[
7
]
.
∎Now, for the case
E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)>E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\geq\frac{1}{2}e^{-n[E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\epsilon]}\right]+\mathbb{P}\left[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\leq e^{-n{[E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\epsilon]}}\right]

(775)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{1+\kappa}}+\mathbb{P}\left[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\leq e^{-n{[E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\epsilon]}}\right]

(776)

where (
776
) follows from
[
15
, Eq. (22)]
with
\gamma_{n}=n^{1+\kappa^{\prime}}
for some
\kappa^{\prime}>0
. Next, we bound the second term in (
776
) for large values of
n
.

Let
\Delta>0
small enough and define

\displaystyle P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\argmin_{P_{XX^{\prime}}:D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}R+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime}),

(777)

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}

\displaystyle=\left[\left|\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})-E\left[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})\right]\right|>2^{-n(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R+\Delta)}\right].

(778)

Then, on
\mathcal{A}^{c}
, by Lemma
24
, we have

\displaystyle 2\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})g_{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R)}g_{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})

(779)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\rm B}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}(x,x^{\prime})},

(780)

where (
779
) follows from Lemma
4
, and (
780
) follows from Lemma
7
and Lemma
6
which proves that

D(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}\|Q_{X}Q_{X^{\prime}})=2R

for the case
E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)>E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\leq 2^{-n{(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\epsilon)}}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{2}{M}\sum_{P_{XX^{\prime}}}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})g_{N}{(P_{XX^{\prime}})}\leq 2^{-n{(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\epsilon)}}\right]

(781)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{2}{M}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})g_{N}{(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})}\leq 2^{-n{(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\epsilon)}}\right]

(782)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{2}{M}\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})g_{N}{(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})}\leq 2^{-n{(R+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}(x,x^{\prime})+\epsilon)}}\right]

(783)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\left[2\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})g_{N}{(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})}\leq 2^{-n{(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}(x,x^{\prime})+\epsilon)}}\right]

(784)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\left[2\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})g_{N}{(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})}\leq 2^{-n{(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}(x,x^{\prime})+\epsilon)}}\bigg|\mathcal{A}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A})

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{P}\left[2\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})g_{N}{(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})}\leq 2^{-n{(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}(x,x^{\prime})+\epsilon)}}\bigg|\mathcal{A}^{c}\right]\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}^{c})

(785)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A})+\mathbb{P}\left[2\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})g_{N}{(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*})}\leq 2^{-n{(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}(x,x^{\prime})+\epsilon)}}\bigg|\mathcal{A}^{c}\right]

(786)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2^{-n\Delta^{\prime}}+0

(787)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{-n\Delta^{\prime}},

(788)

for
n
sufficiently large, where (
783
) follows from Lemma
7
and
E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)>E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
, (
787
) follows from Lemma
24
and (
780
), respectively.

Hence, for this case, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})>\frac{1}{2}2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-\varepsilon)}\bigg]+\mathbb{P}\bigg[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})<2^{-n(E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+\varepsilon)}\bigg]\leq\frac{1}{n^{1+\kappa^{\prime}}}+2^{-n\Delta^{\prime}}

(789)

for some
\kappa^{\prime}>0
and
\Delta^{\prime}>0
.

Finally, from (
767
) and (
789
), our proof is concluded.

A-H

Proof of Lemma
9

Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]

\displaystyle\geq\mathbb{E}[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]

(790)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{P}\bigg(\bigcup_{j\neq i}\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}\bigg)\bigg]

(791)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{1}\bigg\{\bigcup_{j\neq i}\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}\bigg\}\bigg]\bigg]

(792)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\frac{\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}]\big]\big)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}]]+\sum_{k\neq i,j}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}\{\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{k}\}\}]]}

(793)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{M_{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}\frac{\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}]\big]\big)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}]]+\sum_{k\neq i,j}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{k}\}]}

(794)

\displaystyle=\frac{(M_{n}-1)\big(\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}]\big]\big)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}]]+(M_{n}-2)\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]}

(795)

\displaystyle=\frac{P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}]\big]}{\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}]\big]+(M_{n}-2)\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]\big]},

(796)

where (
793
) follows from Caen’s inequality in Lemma
2
by, for each fixed
i
, setting
\mathcal{I}_{i}=\{j\in[M]\setminus\{i\}:j\neq i\},A_{j}^{(i)}=\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}
with the probability measure defined as

\mathbb{P}(A_{j}^{(i)})=\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{1}\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}]]=\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}]],
where the inner expectation is over the BSC channel randomness and the outer one is over the random codebook ensemble. This is the probability of event
\{\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}\}
on the a product probability space generated from channel statistics and random codebook generations. By the symmetry of the codebook generation, it is easy to see that
\mathbb{P}(A_{j}^{(i)})=\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}]]=\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}]]=\mathbb{P}(A_{2}^{(1)})
for all
j\neq i
.

From (
796
), it holds that

\displaystyle 1

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]}{\mathbb{E}[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]}

(797)

\displaystyle\leq 1+(M_{n}-2)\frac{\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]}{\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}]]}.

(798)

Recall the definition of
d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})
in (
746
). Assume that
\bm{x}_{1}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})
for some
P_{X}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X})
, which is a fixed vector. Then, given
(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})
and
(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{"}})
where
P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
and
P_{XX^{"}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\{\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{x}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{x}_{3}\}|(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}}),(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{"}})\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\min\bigg\{\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{x}_{2}|(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\},\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{x}_{3}|(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{"}})\}\bigg\}

(799)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\min\bigg\{2^{-n\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})},2^{-n\sum_{x,x"}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x")P_{XX"}(x,x")}\bigg\}

(800)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{-n\max\big\{\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime}),\sum_{x,x"}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x")P_{XX"}(x,x")\big\}},

(801)

which does not depend on
\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2},\bm{x}_{3}
, where (
800
) follows from Lemma
4
.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{x}_{2}}\mathbb{P}(\bm{x}_{2})\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}

(802)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{x}_{2}}2^{-n\big(H(P_{X}^{\prime})+D(P_{X^{\prime}}\|Q)\big)}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}

(803)

\displaystyle=2^{-n\big(H(P_{X}^{\prime})+D(P_{X^{\prime}}\|Q)\big)}\sum_{\bm{x}_{2}}\mathbf{1}\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{x}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}

(804)

\displaystyle=2^{-n\big(H(P_{X}^{\prime})+D(P_{X^{\prime}}\|Q)\big)}\frac{|\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}}|}{|\mathcal{T}(P_{X})|}

(805)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n\big(H(P_{X}^{\prime})+D(P_{X^{\prime}}\|Q)\big)}\frac{2^{NH(P_{XX^{\prime}})|}}{2^{NH(P_{X})|}}

(806)

\displaystyle=2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)},

(807)

where (
803
) and (
806
) follow from

[
29
]
.

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{"}})\bigg]=2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{"})+D(P_{X}^{"}\|Q)\big)}.

(808)

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\cap\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{"}})\}\bigg|\bm{X}_{1}=\bm{x}_{1}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\bigg[\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\cap\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{"}})\}\bigg]

(809)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\bigg]\mathbb{P}\bigg[(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX"})\bigg]

(810)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)}2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{"})+D(P_{X}^{"}\|Q)\big)}

(811)

\displaystyle\qquad=2^{-n(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+I_{P}(X;X^{"})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)+D(P_{X^{"}}\|Q))},

(812)

where (
811
) follows from (
807
) and (
808
).

It follows from (
801
) and (
812
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{X}}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{P_{X^{\prime}|X}}\sum_{P_{X^{"}|X}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{P}\{\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}\bigg|(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}_{P_{XX^{\prime}}},(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}_{P_{XX^{"}}}\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\mathbb{P}\bigg[\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{2})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\cap\{(\bm{x}_{1},\bm{X}_{3})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{"}})\}\bigg|\bm{X}_{1}=\bm{x}_{1}\bigg]\bigg]

(813)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\sum_{P_{X^{\prime}|X}}\sum_{P_{X^{"}|X}}2^{-n\max\{\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime}),\sum_{x,x"}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x")P_{XX^{"}}(x,x")\}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times 2^{-n(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+I_{P}(X;X^{"})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)+D(P_{X}^{"}\|Q))}

(814)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{P_{X^{\prime}|X}}\sum_{P_{X^{"}|X}}2^{-\frac{n}{2}\big(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x"}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x")P_{XX"}(x,x")\big)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times 2^{-n(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+I_{P}(X;X^{"})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)+D(P_{X^{"}}\|Q))}

(815)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{P_{X^{\prime}|X}}2^{-\frac{n}{2}\big(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigg(\sum_{P_{X^{"}|X}}2^{-\frac{n}{2}\big(\sum_{x,x"}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x")P_{XX^{"}}(x,x")\big)}2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{"})+D(P_{X^{"}}\|Q)\big)}\bigg)

(816)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{P_{X^{\prime}|X}}2^{-\frac{n}{2}\big(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)}\bigg)^{2},

(817)

where (
815
) follows from
\max\{a,b\}\geq\frac{a+b}{2}
.

It follows from (
817
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{\bm{x}_{1}}\mathbb{P}(\bm{x}_{1})\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]\big|\bm{X}_{1}=\bm{x}_{1}]

(818)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{\bm{x}_{1}}\mathbb{P}(\bm{x}_{1})\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]

(819)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{P_{X}}\sum_{\bm{x}_{1}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})}\mathbb{P}(\bm{x}_{1})\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]

(820)

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{P_{X}}\sum_{\bm{x}_{1}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})}2^{-n(D(P_{X}\|Q)+H(P_{X}))}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{x}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]

(821)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{P_{X}}\sum_{\bm{x}_{1}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})}2^{-n(D(P_{X}\|Q)+H(P_{X}))}\bigg(\sum_{P_{X^{\prime}|X}}2^{-\frac{n}{2}\big(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)}\bigg)^{2}

(822)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{P_{X}}2^{-nD(P_{X}\|Q)}\bigg(\sum_{P_{X^{\prime}|X}}2^{-\frac{n}{2}\big(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}2^{-n\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)}\bigg)^{2},

(823)

where (
819
) follows from the independence of codewords in the random codebook ensemble, (
821
) follows from
[
29
]
.

Now, from (
772
) in the proof of Lemma
24
, for all joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
such that
D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X^{\prime}})>2R
, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{P}\big[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\geq 1\big]\leq\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}2^{-n\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-2R\big)}<\infty.

(824)

From (
824
) and Borel-Cantelli’s lemma
[
23
]
, it holds almost surely that

\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})=0
for all joint type
P_{XX^{\prime}}
such that
D(Q_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X^{\prime}})>2R
.

Hence, from (
817
) and the above fact with noting the number of types or conditional types are sub-exponential in
N
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}]]

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n\big(\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}D(P_{X}\|Q)+2\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)}

(825)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n\big(\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}D(P_{X}\|Q)+2\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\big)},

(826)

where (
825
) follows from the sub-exponential number of possible
n
-types in
\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}

[
29
]
, and (
826
) follows from the fact that
\mathcal{P}_{n}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
is dense in
\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X})
.

Now, note that
Q_{X}=Q_{X}^{\prime}=Q
, so we have

\displaystyle I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})

\displaystyle=D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|P_{X}P_{X}^{\prime})

(827)

\displaystyle=D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-D(P_{X}\|Q)-D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q).

(828)

It follows that

\displaystyle D(P_{X}\|Q)+2\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)

\displaystyle\qquad=D(P_{X}\|Q)+2\big(D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-D(P_{X}\|Q)\big)

(829)

\displaystyle\qquad=2D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-D(P_{X}\|Q)

(830)

\displaystyle\qquad\geq D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}),

(831)

where (
831
) follows from the data processing for KL divergence (or log-sum inequality
[
34
]
).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}D(P_{X}\|Q)+2\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

(832)

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)+R,

(833)

where (
832
) follows from (
831
), and (
833
) follows from Lemma
7
. Note that (
832
) becomes equality if and only if
P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})=Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})
for all
x,x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}
. However, at
P_{XX^{\prime}}=Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}D(P_{X}\|Q)+2\big(I_{P}(X;X^{\prime})+D(P_{X}^{\prime}\|Q)\big)+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})

(834)

\displaystyle\qquad=-\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}\log\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}\bigg)Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})

(835)

\displaystyle\qquad>-\log\bigg(\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\bigg)

(836)

\displaystyle\qquad=-\log\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}\sqrt{W(y|x)W(y|x^{\prime})}Q(x)Q(x^{\prime})\bigg)

(837)

\displaystyle\qquad=-\log\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\bigg(\sum_{x}\sqrt{W(y|x)}Q(x)\bigg)^{2}\bigg)

(838)

\displaystyle\qquad=-\log\bigg(\sum_{y\in\mathcal{Y}}\bigg(\sum_{x}\sqrt{W(y|x)}Q(x)\bigg)^{2}\bigg)

(839)

\displaystyle\qquad=R_{0}(Q)

(840)

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)+R,

(841)

where (
836
) follows from the convexity of the function
-\log x
with noting that the equality does not happen by the condition (
836
), and (
840
) follows from
[
28
, Eq. (8.45)]
with
R_{0}(Q)
is the cut-off rate of the DMC at the distribution
Q
.

Therefore, from (
833
) and (
841
), we have

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})>E_{\rm trc}(R,Q)+R

(842)

for the case
E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)=E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
.

Now, for the case
E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)>E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)
, (
833
) happens at the optimizer
P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}
satisfying
D(P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})=2R
, which leads to
P_{XX^{\prime}}^{*}\neq Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}
if
R>0
, so the equality can not happen in (
831
).

In summary, at
R>0
and a fixed underlying distribution
Q
, it holds that

\displaystyle\min_{P_{XX^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{X}):D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})\leq 2R}D(P_{XX^{\prime}}\|Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\sum_{x,x^{\prime}}d_{\mathrm{B}}(x,x^{\prime})P_{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})>E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)+R.

(843)

Hence, it holds from (
826
) and (
56
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{P}\big[\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}\}\cap\{\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{3}\}\bigg]\leq 2\times 2^{-\big(R+E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)\big)n}2^{-\delta(R)n}

(844)

for some constant
\delta(R)>0
.

Now, on the other hand, we know that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{1}\to\bm{X}_{2}]]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}(M_{n}-1)}\sum_{i=0}^{M_{n}-1}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{E}[\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}]]

(845)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{M_{n}-1}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\frac{1}{M_{n}}\sum_{i=0}^{M_{n}-1}\sum_{j\neq i}\mathbb{P}[\bm{X}_{i}\to\bm{X}_{j}]\bigg]

(846)

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathbb{E}[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]}{M_{n}-1}

(847)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\mathbb{E}[P_{\rm e}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]}{M_{n}-1}

(848)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-n(E_{\rm rce}(R,Q)-R)}.

(849)

From (
798
), (
844
), and (
849
), we obtain

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}[P_{\mathrm{e}}^{\rm ub}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]}{\mathbb{E}[P_{\mathrm{e}}(\mathcal{C}_{n})]}-1

(850)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-N(\delta(R)+E_{\rm trc}^{\rm ub}(R,Q)-E_{\rm rce}(R,Q))}.

(851)

A-I

Proof of Lemma
11

Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[2^{-tS_{n}/n}\big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}2^{-\frac{t(x_{1}+x_{2}+\cdots+x_{n})}{n}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]

(852)

\displaystyle=\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}2^{-\frac{t(x_{1}+x_{2}+\cdots+x_{n})}{n}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\mathbf{1}\{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}2^{-\frac{t(x_{1}+x_{2}+\cdots+x_{n})}{n}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\mathbf{1}\{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}\}

(853)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}2^{-\frac{t(x_{1}+x_{2}+\cdots+x_{n})}{n}}\prod_{k=1}^{n}P(x_{k})\mathbf{1}\{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}2^{-\frac{t(x_{1}+x_{2}+\cdots+x_{n})}{n}}\mathbf{1}\{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}\}

(854)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}2^{-\frac{t(x_{1}+x_{2}+\cdots+x_{n})}{n}}\prod_{k=1}^{n}P(x_{k})+\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}2^{-n\zeta}\mathbf{1}\{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}\}

(855)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\prod_{i=1}^{n}P(x_{i})}\big[2^{-tS_{n}/n}\big]+2^{-n\zeta}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|.

(856)

A-J

Proof of Lemma
12

We have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{I}\{i,j\}\mathcal{I}\{i,k\}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\{(X_{i},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}}),(X_{i},X_{k})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}

(857)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{x}_{i}}\mathbb{P}\{X_{i}=\bm{x}_{i}\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x}_{i},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}}),(\bm{x}_{i},X_{k})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}

(858)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{x}_{i}}\mathbb{P}\{X_{i}=\bm{x}_{i}\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x}_{i},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x}_{i},X_{k})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}

(859)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\bm{x}_{i}}\mathbb{P}\{X_{i}=\bm{x}_{i}\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x}_{i},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}^{2}

(860)

\displaystyle=\sum_{P_{X}}\mathcal{N}(P_{X})\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}^{2}

(861)

\displaystyle\dot{=}\max_{P_{X}}\mathcal{N}(P_{X})\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}^{2}

(862)

where in (
858
) we conditioned to codeword
X_{i}
being equal to a given realization
\bm{x}_{i}
, (
859
) is because
X_{j}
and
X_{k}
are independent, (
860
) is because they are also identically distributed, in (
861
) we grouped codewords
X_{i}
according to their type
P_{X}
and used the fact that
\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x}_{i},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}
takes the same value when
\bm{x}_{i}
has the same type. Expression (
862
) is hard to calculate because of the term
\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}
. Therefore we find a lower bound and an upper bound on Eqn. (
861
). The lower bound is:

\displaystyle\sum_{P_{X}}\mathcal{N}(P_{X})\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\left(\sum_{P_{X}}\mathcal{N}(P_{X})\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\right)^{2}

(863)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\{(X_{i},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}^{2}

(864)

\displaystyle\qquad\dot{=}2^{-n2D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(865)

while the upper bound is:

\displaystyle\sum_{P_{X}}\mathcal{N}(P_{X})\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{P_{X}}\mathcal{N}(P_{X})\mathbb{P}\{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\cdot\max_{P_{X}}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}

(866)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\{(X_{i},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\}\max_{P_{X}}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}

(867)

\displaystyle\qquad\dot{=}2^{-n[D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})+\eta]}

(868)

where

\eta=-\frac{1}{n}\log\max_{P_{X}}\mathbb{P}\{(\bm{x},X_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(P_{XX^{\prime}})|\bm{x}\in\mathcal{T}(P_{X})\}\leq D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}),

and the inequality follows from (
865
).

A-K

Proof of Lemma
13

The proof is based on
[
35
, Th. 10]
. A similar proof of an equivalent result is presented for the case of constant composition codes in
[
12
]
. However, there are several differences with our case. First of all our Lemma
12
gives a bound rather than a dot equality, which has implications on the minimum exponent starting from which a double exponential decay is found. Other differences with
[
12
]
are indicated in the following.

Let us define the quantities:

\displaystyle a=2^{-n\epsilon}

(869)

\displaystyle\Delta=\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]\dot{=}2^{n(2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}))}.

(870)

Let us consider a graph in which vertices are indicated with pairs (e.g.,
(k,l)
is a vertex). Two vertices
(k,l)
and
(i,j)
are connected if exactly one index in both pairs coincide. Let us indicate with
(k,l)\sim(i,j)
the case in which vertices
(k,l)
and
(i,j)
are connected. Let us also indicate with
[M]_{*}^{2}
the set
\{i,j\in\{1,2,\ldots M_{n}\}:i\neq j\}
.
Let us define and bound the following quantity:

\displaystyle\Theta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{(i,j)\in[M]_{*}^{2}}\sum_{(k,l)\in[M]_{*}^{2},(k,l)\sim(i,j)}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{I}\{i,j\}\mathcal{I}\{i,k\}]

(871)

\displaystyle\dot{=}\frac{1}{2}2^{2nR}(2^{nr}+2^{nr}-2)\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{I}\{1,2\}\mathcal{I}\{1,3\}]

(872)

\displaystyle\dot{\leq}\frac{1}{2}2^{2nR}(2^{nr}+2^{nr}-2)2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(873)

\displaystyle\dot{=}\frac{1}{2}2^{n(3R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}))}

(874)

where (
872
) and (
873
) follow from Lemma
12
as well as from the fact that codewords are i.i.d. and noticing that there are about
M_{n}^{2}
codeword pairs
(i,j)
and, for each of them, there are exactly
2^{nR}-1
connected vertices. Note that in
[
12
]
a dot equality rather than an inequality is present.

Now let us define and, where needed, bound the following three quantities:

\displaystyle\Omega

\displaystyle=\max_{(i,j)\in[M]^{2}_{*}}\sum_{(k,l)\in[M]_{*}^{2},(k,l)\sim(i,j)}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{I}\{i,k\}]

(875)

\displaystyle\dot{=}(2^{nr}+2^{nr}-2)2^{-nD(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})}

(876)

\displaystyle\dot{=}2^{n(R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime}))}

(877)

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta}{6\Omega}\dot{=}\frac{2^{n[2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}}{2^{n[R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}}=2^{nR}

(878)

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta^{2}}{8\Theta+2\Delta}

\displaystyle\dot{\geq}\frac{2^{n[4R-2D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}}{e^{n[3R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}+2^{n[2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}}

(879)

\displaystyle=\frac{2^{n[2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}}{2^{nR}+1}

(880)

\displaystyle\dot{=}2^{n[R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}

(881)

Using the definitions above and the result in
[
35
, Th. 10]
we obtain:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\leq 2^{-n\epsilon}\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})]\right]

\displaystyle\dot{=}\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{N}(P_{XX^{\prime}})\leq 2^{n[2R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})-\epsilon]}\right]

(883)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle.}}{{\leq}}\exp\Bigl\{-\min\left(2^{n[R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]},2^{nR}\right)\Bigr\}

(884)

\displaystyle=\exp\Bigl\{-2^{n[R-D(P_{XX^{\prime}}||Q_{X}Q_{X}^{\prime})]}\Bigr\}

(885)

which concludes the proof of the lemma.

A-L

Proof of Lemma
14

We prove by induction. The condition (
294
) shows that (
295
) holds for
n=2
,
\forall A_{1},A_{2}\subset\mathcal{X}
. Now, assume that (
295
) holds for some
n\geq 2
. We need to show that (
295
) holds for
n+1
.
Indeed, let
A_{1},A_{2},\cdots,A_{n},A_{n+1}\in\mathcal{X}
such that there exists
i,j\in[n]
with
i\neq j
such that
A_{i}\cap A_{j}=\emptyset
.

Now, by reordering
\{A_{k}\}_{k=1}^{n}
, we can assume without loss of generality that
i,j\in[n]
. This leads to
\cap_{k=1}^{n}A_{k}=\emptyset
.
Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigcap_{k=1}^{n+1}\big\{X\in\mathcal{A}_{k}\big\}\bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigcap_{k=1}^{n}\big\{X\in\mathcal{A}_{k}\big\}\bigg]\mathbb{P}\bigg[X\in\mathcal{A}_{n+1}\bigg|X\in\bigcap_{k=1}^{n}A_{k}\bigg]

(886)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{P}\bigg[\bigcap_{k=1}^{n}\big\{X\in\mathcal{A}_{k}\big\}\bigg]\mathbb{P}\bigg[X\in\mathcal{A}_{n+1}\bigg]

(887)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\beta\prod_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}[X\in\mathcal{A}_{k}]\mathbb{P}\big[X\in\mathcal{A}_{n+1}\big]

(888)

\displaystyle\qquad=\beta\prod_{k=1}^{n+1}\mathbb{P}[X\in\mathcal{A}_{k}].

(889)

This concludes our proof by induction.

A-M

Proof of Lemma
15

We consider four cases:

•

Case 1:
a=1,b=1
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=a\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=b\}\big]=0.

(890)

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})\big]\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}})\big]

(891)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})},

(892)

where (
892
) follows from
[
12
]
.
Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]

(893)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-nI_{\min}}(Q)}\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big],

(894)

hence, (
296
) holds for this case.

•

Case 2:
a=1,b=0
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}[\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})\}\cap\{\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\not\in\mathcal{T}(\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}})\}\}]

(895)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})]

(896)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}.

(897)

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})\big]\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\not\in\mathcal{T}(\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}})\big]

(898)

\displaystyle\doteq 2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big(1-2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big).

(899)

Now, there are two sub-cases. If
I_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})=0
, from (
897
) and (
899
) we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]

(900)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-nI_{\min}}(Q)}\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big].

(901)

On the other hand, if
I_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})>0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\big]
