\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}}\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]

(902)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-nI_{\min}}(Q)}\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big],

(903)

where (
903
) follows from
I_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})\geq I_{\min}(Q)
.

•

Case 3:
a=0,b=1
. Similarly as case 2, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]

(904)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-nI_{\min}}(Q)}\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big].

(905)

•

Case 4:
a=0,b=0
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}[\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\notin\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})\}\cap\{\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\notin\mathcal{T}(\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}})\}\}]

(906)

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\notin\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})\big]+\mathbb{P}\big[(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\notin\mathcal{T}(\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}})\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{P}\big[\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\notin\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})\}\cup\{\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\notin\mathcal{T}(\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}})\}\}\big]

(907)

\displaystyle\qquad=\big(1-2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big)+\big(1-2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-(1-\mathbb{P}\big[\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(Q_{XX^{\prime}})\}\cap\{(\bm{X}_{i},\bm{X}_{j})\in\mathcal{T}(\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}})\}\big])

(908)

\displaystyle\qquad=\big(1-2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big)+\big(1-2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big)-1

(909)

\displaystyle\qquad=1-2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}-2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}.

(910)

On the other hand, we also have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]

\displaystyle\qquad=\big(1-2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big)\big(1-2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}\big)

(911)

\displaystyle\qquad=1-2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}-2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}+2^{-nI_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}2^{-nI_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}(X;X^{\prime})}.

(912)

From (
910
) and (
912
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\big[\{Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\cap\{Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\}\big]\leq\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big]

(913)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-nI_{\min}}(Q)}\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{Q_{XX^{\prime}}}=1\big]\mathbb{P}\big[Z_{\tilde{Q}_{XX^{\prime}}}=0\big].

(914)

From the four cases above, we finally obtain (
296
)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|X_{i}^{3}|]\to 0,

(915)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}^{4}]\to 0,

(916)

A-N

Proof of Lemma
16

The proof is based on a modification of a proof based on Stein’s method in
[
25
, Lemma 3.2]
. Without loss of generality, (or by scaling), we can assume that
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}^{2}]=n
.

Let

\displaystyle T=\frac{S_{n}}{\sqrt{n}},

(917)

and

\displaystyle T_{i}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{j\neq i}X_{j},\quad\forall i\in[n].

(918)

Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[Tf(T)]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}(f(T)-f(T_{i}))-(T-T_{i})f^{\prime}(T)\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}(T-T_{i})f^{\prime}(T)\bigg]+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}f(T_{i})\bigg].

(919)

Now, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[Tf(T)-f^{\prime}(T)]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}\big(f(T)-f(T_{i})-(T-T_{i})f^{\prime}(T)\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}(T-T_{i})f^{\prime}(T)\bigg]+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}f(T_{i})\bigg]-\mathbb{E}[f^{\prime}(T)]

(920)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}\big(f(T)-f(T_{i})-(T-T_{i})f^{\prime}(T)\big)\bigg]\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}f(T_{i})\bigg]\bigg|+\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[f^{\prime}(T)\bigg(1-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}(T-T_{i})\bigg)\bigg]\bigg|

(921)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\|f^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\|}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}(T-T_{i})^{2}|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}f(T_{i})\bigg]\bigg|+\frac{\|f^{\prime}\|}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-X_{i}^{2})\bigg|

(922)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{\|f^{{}^{\prime\prime}}\|}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}|X_{i}^{3}|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg|\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i}f(T_{i})\bigg]\bigg|+\frac{\|f^{\prime}\|}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-X_{i}^{2})\bigg|.

(923)

Now, observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}f(T_{i})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}f(t_{i})

(924)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}\in\mathcal{V}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}f(t_{i})

(925)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}f(t_{i})

(926)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}\in\mathcal{V}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}f(t_{i})

(927)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}f(t_{i})

(928)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}f(t_{i}).

(929)

From (
929
), we obtain

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}f(T_{i})]\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}f(t_{i})\bigg|

(930)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|f\|_{\infty}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{N}\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\mathbb{P}[X_{1}=x_{1},X_{2}=x_{2},\cdots,X_{n}=x_{n}]\bigg|\sum_{k=1}^{n}x_{k}\bigg|

(931)

\displaystyle=\frac{\|f\|_{\infty}}{\sqrt{n}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}\bigg|

(932)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|f\|_{\infty}}{\sqrt{n}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}x_{k}^{4}

(933)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|f\|_{\infty}}{\sqrt{n}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|g(n)\to 0

(934)

as
n\to\infty
.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-X_{i}^{2})\bigg|\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=x_{i})\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-x_{i}^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-x_{i}^{2})\bigg|

(935)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=x_{i})\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-x_{i}^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\max\bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}^{2},1\bigg\}

(936)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=x_{i})\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-x_{i}^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{n\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}^{4}},1\bigg\}

(937)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=x_{i})\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-x_{i}^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\max\bigg\{\sqrt{ng(n)},1\bigg\}

(938)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=x_{i})\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-x_{i}^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+|\mathcal{V}^{c}|g(n)\bigg(\frac{1}{ng(n)}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{ng(n)},1\bigg\}\bigg)

(939)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=x_{i})\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1-x_{i}^{2})\bigg|+o(1)

(940)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg)\frac{\|f^{\prime\prime}\|_{\infty}}{2n^{3/2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|X_{k}|^{3}]+o(1),

(941)

where (
941
) follows from
[
25
, Proof of Lemma 3.4]
.

Furthermore, we also have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\big[|X_{i}|^{3}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}[X_{i}=x_{i}]\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)

(942)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}[X_{i}=x_{i}]\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{1/2}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)

(943)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}[X_{i}=x_{i}]\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{1/2}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\sup_{(x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n})\in\mathcal{V}^{c}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{4}\bigg)^{3/4}

(944)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}[X_{i}=x_{i}]\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{1/2}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|\bigg(\frac{g(n)}{n}\bigg)^{3/4}

(945)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}[X_{i}=x_{i}]\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{1/2}}|\mathcal{V}^{c}|g(n)\frac{1}{(g(n)n^{5})^{1/4}}

(946)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n^{3/2}}\bigg)\sum_{x_{1},x_{2},\cdots,x_{n}}\prod_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{P}[X_{i}=x_{i}]\bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}|x_{i}|^{3}\bigg)+o(1)

(947)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg)\frac{\|f^{\prime\prime}\|_{\infty}}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}^{4}]},

(948)

where (
944
) follows from the concavity of the function
x^{3/4}
on
(0,\infty)
.

From (
923
), (
934
), (
940
), and (
948
), we obtain

\displaystyle\big|\mathbb{E}[f^{\prime}(T)-Tf(T)]\big|\leq\bigg(\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg)\frac{\|f^{\prime\prime}\|_{\infty}}{2n^{3/2}}\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[|X_{k}|^{3}]+\bigg(\frac{1}{1-2^{-f(n)}}\bigg)\frac{\|f^{\prime\prime}\|_{\infty}}{n}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{k}^{4}]}+o(1)\to 0

(949)

as
n\to\infty
under the conditions (
310
) and (
311
).

Then, by
[
25
, Th. 3.1]
, we conclude that

\displaystyle T\smash{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle({\rm d})}}{{\,\longrightarrow\,}}}\mathcal{N}(0,1),

(950)

under the conditions (
310
) and (
311
). Now, since
\tilde{T}
is a scaling of
T
, hence by Slutsky’s theorem, it holds that

\displaystyle\tilde{T}\smash{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle({\rm d})}}{{\,\longrightarrow\,}}}\bigg(\lim_{n\to\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\var(S_{n})}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}^{2}]}}\bigg)\mathcal{N}(0,1).

(951)

Now, since
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{T}]=0
and
\var(\tilde{T})=1
, by applying Lemma
1
with
\var(Z)=1
, we must have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\var(S_{n})}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[X_{i}^{2}]}}=1,

(952)

which leads to

\displaystyle\tilde{T}\smash{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle({\rm d})}}{{\,\longrightarrow\,}}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle(d)}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}(0,1).

(953)

A-O

Proof of Lemma
17

This proof is based on the proof of
[
25
, Prop. 1.2]
. Consider the function
h_{x}(w)=\mathbf{1}\{w\leq x\}
, and the ‘smooth’
h_{x,\varepsilon}(w)
defined to be one for
w\leq x
, zero for
w>x+\varepsilon
, and linear between them. Then, it is clear that
h_{x,\varepsilon}\in\mathcal{V}
with
a=x
and
c=\varepsilon
.

First, observe that
\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(w)
is
1
-Lipschitz and

\displaystyle\big\|\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}\big\|_{\infty}\leq\varepsilon.

(954)

Hence, it holds that

\displaystyle 4\sqrt{2\pi}h_{x,4\sqrt{2\pi}}\in\mathcal{H}=\{h\in\mathcal{V}:c\leq 4\sqrt{2\pi}\},

(955)

so
\mathcal{H}
in the definition of Wasserstein metric (cf. Definition
2
) is a non-empty set, and
d_{W}(T,Z)
is well-defined.

Furthermore, by definition of
d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)
, it holds that

\displaystyle d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\mathbb{E}[|h(Z)|]+\mathbb{E}[|h(T)|]

(956)

\displaystyle\leq 2\|h\|_{\infty}

(957)

\displaystyle=2c

(958)

\displaystyle\leq 8\sqrt{2\pi}.

(959)

Now, by setting
\varepsilon=(2\pi)^{1/4}\sqrt{2d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}\big\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq(2\pi)^{1/4}\sqrt{2d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)}

(960)

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{2\pi},

(961)

where (
960
) follows from (
954
), and (
961
) follows from (
959
).
This means that
\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}\in\mathcal{H}
since
\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}\in\mathcal{V}
as mentioned above.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(Z)]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]+\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(Z)]

(962)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]+\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(Z)]

(963)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\bigg(\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\bigg)+\big|\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(Z)]\big|

(964)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|+\big|\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(Z)]\big|.

(965)

Similarly, by choosing
h_{x,\varepsilon}(\omega)
to be
1
when
\omega\leq x-\varepsilon
,
0
when
\omega\geq x
, and linear between them, which is also a function in
\mathcal{V}
, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(Z)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(T)]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|+\big|\mathbb{E}[h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]-\mathbb{E}[h_{x}(Z)]\big|

(966)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{x}^{x+\varepsilon}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\exp\bigg(-\frac{z^{2}}{2}\bigg)\big(h_{x,\varepsilon}(z)-h_{x}(z)\big)dz

(967)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|+\frac{\varepsilon}{2\sqrt{2\pi}}.

(968)

From (
965
) and (
966
), we obtain

\displaystyle\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|+\frac{\varepsilon}{2\sqrt{2\pi}}.

(969)

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\big|\mathbb{P}(-T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(-T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|+\frac{\varepsilon}{2\sqrt{2\pi}}.

(970)

It follows from (
969
) and (
970
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}}\min\{\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(-T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\min\bigg\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|,\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\big|\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(-T)]-\mathbb{E}[\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}(Z)]\big|\bigg\}+\frac{\varepsilon}{2\sqrt{2\pi}}

(971)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{\varepsilon}d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)+\frac{\varepsilon}{2\sqrt{2\pi}}

(972)

\displaystyle\qquad=(8\pi)^{-1/4}\sqrt{d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)},

(973)

where (
972
) follows from
\varepsilon h_{x,\varepsilon}\in\mathcal{H}
, and (
973
) follows from our setting
\varepsilon=(2\pi)^{1/4}\sqrt{2d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)}
above.

Now, for any
x\in\mathbb{R}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}}\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq-x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq-x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\bigg\}

(974)

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|-\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(T\geq-x)\big|\bigg\}

(975)

\displaystyle\qquad\geq\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\bigg\}-\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(T\geq-x)\big|,

(976)

where (
975
) follows from the triangle inequality.

From (
973
) and (
976
), we obtain (
396
).

Now, if the distribution of
T
is tight, then there exists a distribution
\tilde{Y}
such that
T\smash{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle({\rm d})}}{{\,\longrightarrow\,}}}\tilde{Y}

[
23
]
. Then, if
x
is a continuous point of
\mathbb{P}(\tilde{Y}\leq x)
such that
x\to 0
as
n\to\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq-x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad=\lim_{n\to\infty}\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{P}(\tilde{Y}\leq x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(\tilde{Y}\leq-x)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq x)\big|\bigg\}

(977)

\displaystyle\qquad=\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{P}(\tilde{Y}\leq 0)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq 0)\big|,\big|\mathbb{P}(\tilde{Y}\leq 0)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq 0)\big|\bigg\}

(978)

\displaystyle\qquad=\lim_{n\to\infty}\big|\mathbb{P}(T\leq 0)-\mathbb{P}(Z\leq 0)\big|,

(979)

where (
977
) follows from
\lim_{N\to\infty}\min\{A_{n},B_{n}\}=\min\{\lim_{n\to\infty}A_{n},\lim_{n\to\infty}B_{n}\}
if both the limits
\lim_{n\to\infty}A_{n}
and
\lim_{N\to\infty}B_{n}
exist.

Hence, we obtain (
397
) from (
979
) and (
973
).

A-P

Proof of Lemma
19

By Lemma
18
, we have

\displaystyle d_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)\leq\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{E}\big[f_{h}^{\prime}(T)-Tf_{h}(T)\big]\big|,\big|\mathbb{E}\big[f_{h}^{\prime}(-T)+Tf_{h}(-T)\big]\big|\bigg\}.

(980)

Now, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[f_{h}^{\prime}(T)-Tf_{h}(T)\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[h(T)]-\mathbb{E}[h(Z)]

(981)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[h(T_{1})]-\mathbb{E}[h(Z)]+\mathbb{E}[h(T)-h(T_{1})]

(982)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(T_{1})-T_{1}f_{h}(T_{1})]+\mathbb{E}[h(T)-h(T_{1})],

(983)

where (
981
) and (
983
) follow from (
398
).

It follows that

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\big[f_{h}^{\prime}(T)-Tf_{h}(T)\big]\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(T_{1})-T_{1}f_{h}(T_{1})]+\mathbb{E}[h(T)-h(T_{1})]\bigg|

(984)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(T_{1})-T_{1}f_{h}(T_{1})]\bigg|+\bigg|\mathbb{E}[h(T)-h(T_{1})]\bigg|

(985)

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(T_{1})-T_{1}f_{h}(T_{1})]\bigg|+\mathbb{E}[h(T)-h(T_{1})]

(986)

where (
986
) follows from
T\leq T_{1}
and
h
is non-increasing.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{E}\big[f_{h}^{\prime}(-T)+Tf_{h}(-T)\big]\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(-T_{1})+T_{1}f_{h}(-T_{1})]+\mathbb{E}[h(-T)-h(-T_{1})]\bigg|

(987)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(-T_{1})+T_{1}f_{h}(-T_{1})]\bigg|+\mathbb{E}[h(-T_{1})-h(T)],

(988)

where (
988
) follows from
T\leq T_{1}
and
h
is non-increasing.

From (
986
) and (
988
), for all
h\in\mathcal{H}
, we have

\displaystyle\min\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{E}\big[f_{h}^{\prime}(T)-Tf_{h}(T)\big]\big|,\big|\mathbb{E}\big[f_{h}^{\prime}(-T)+Tf_{h}(-T)\big]\big|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\max\bigg\{\big|\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(T_{1})-T_{1}f_{h}(T_{1})]\big|,\big|\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{\prime}(T_{1})-T_{1}f_{h}(T_{1})]\big|\bigg\}+\min\bigg\{\mathbb{E}[h(T)-h(T_{1})],\mathbb{E}[h(-T_{1})-h(-T)]\bigg\}.

(989)

Finally, we obtain (
400
) from (
989
).

A-Q

Proof of Lemma
23

The proof of this lemma is based on the proof of the
[
25
, Th. 3.1]
. Given
h\in\mathcal{H}
, we choose
f_{h}
be a solution of the following ODE equation:

\displaystyle f_{h}^{\prime}(w)-wf_{h}(w)=h(w)-\Phi(h)

(990)

where
\Phi(h)=\mathbb{E}[h(Z)]
with
Z\sim\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, then we have

\displaystyle f_{h}(w)

\displaystyle=e^{\frac{w^{2}}{2}}\int_{w}^{\infty}e^{-\frac{t^{2}}{2}}\big(\Phi(h)-h(t))dt

(991)

\displaystyle=-e^{\frac{w^{2}}{2}}\int_{-\infty}^{w}e^{-\frac{t^{2}}{2}}\big(\Phi(h)-h(t))dt.

(992)

Now, it is easy to prove the following facts from (
992
) (see
[
25
]
):

\displaystyle\|f_{h}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|h^{\prime}\|_{\infty}=2,

(993)

\displaystyle\|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\|h^{\prime}\|_{\infty}=\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}},

(994)

\displaystyle\|f_{h}^{"}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|h^{\prime}\|_{\infty}=2.

(995)

Now, assume that
\mathbb{E}[T]=0
and
\mathbb{E}[T^{2}=1]
.

Furthermore, for any
h\in\mathcal{H}
, from (
990
), it holds that

\displaystyle|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)-Tf_{h}(T)|

\displaystyle=|h(T)-\Phi(h)|

(996)

\displaystyle=|h(T)-\mathbb{E}[h(Z)]|

(997)

\displaystyle\leq 2\|h\|_{\infty}

(998)

\displaystyle\leq 8\sqrt{2\pi}.

(999)

Furthermore, from (
990
), we also have

\displaystyle d_{W}(T,Z)

\displaystyle=\sup_{h\in\mathcal{H}}\big|\mathbb{E}[h(T)]-\mathbb{E}[h(Z)]\big|

(1000)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f_{h}:h\in\mathcal{H}}\big|\mathbb{E}[Tf_{h}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)\big]\big|.

(1001)

Now, for all
f_{h}:h\in\mathcal{H}
, observe that

\displaystyle Tf_{h}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)

\displaystyle\qquad=T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)+Tf_{h}(0)+\big(T^{2}-1\big)f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)+\big(f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)\big).

(1002)

It follows from (
1002
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[Tf_{h}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big[T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big]+f_{h}(0)\mathbb{E}[T]+f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\mathbb{E}\big[T^{2}-1\big]+\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)]

(1003)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big[T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big]+\mathbb{E}[f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)],

(1004)

where (
1004
) follows from the fact that
\mathbb{E}[T]=0
and
\mathbb{E}[T^{2}]=1
.

Hence, from (
1001
) and (
1004
), we have

\displaystyle\tilde{d}_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f_{h}:h\in\mathcal{H}}\big|\mathbb{E}[Tf_{h}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)\big]\big|

(1005)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f_{h}:h\in\mathcal{H}}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|\big]+\mathbb{E}[\big|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)\big|].

(1006)

Now, observe that

\displaystyle\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|

\displaystyle=\big|Tf_{h}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)+f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)-Tf_{h}(0)-T^{2}f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big|

(1007)

\displaystyle=\big|Tf_{h}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)+f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)-Tf_{h}(0)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)+(1-T^{2})f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big|

(1008)

\displaystyle\leq\big|Tf_{h}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)\big|+|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)|+|Tf_{h}(0)|+|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)|+|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)(T^{2}-1)|\big)

(1009)

\displaystyle\leq 8\sqrt{2\pi}+2\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}+2|T|+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}|T^{2}-1|

(1010)

\displaystyle=\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi}+2|T|+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}|T^{2}-1|,

(1011)

where (
1010
) follows from (
993
), (
994
) and (
999
).

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|\big]

\displaystyle\qquad=\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi}+2|T|,\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|\bigg\}

(1012)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|\bigg\}+2|T|+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}|T^{2}-1|,

(1013)

where (
1013
) follows from
\min\{A+B,C\}\leq\min\{A,C\}+B
for all
A,B,C\geq 0
. It follows from (
1013
) that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|\big]\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|\bigg\}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}\big[|T|\big].

(1014)

Now, by Taylor’s expansion, for some
\eta\in(0,-|T|)\cup(0,|T|)
, we have

\displaystyle f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)=\frac{1}{2}f_{h}^{"}(\eta)T^{2},

(1015)

so

\displaystyle\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\big|T^{3}f_{h}^{"}(\eta)\big|

(1016)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|f_{h}^{"}|_{\infty}|T^{3}|

(1017)

\displaystyle\leq|T^{3}|.

(1018)

Hence, from (
1014
) and (
1018
), we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big)\big|\big]\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|^{3}\bigg\}\bigg]+2\mathbb{E}\big[|T|\big]+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}|T^{2}-1|.

(1019)

Similarly, by Taylor’s expansion, for some
\theta\in(0,-|T|)\cup(0,|T|)
, we have

\displaystyle f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)=f_{h}^{"}(\theta)T,

(1020)

so

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\big|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)\big|\big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\big[\big|f_{h}^{"}(\theta)T\big|\big]

(1021)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|f_{h}^{"}(\theta)||T|\big]

(1022)

\displaystyle\leq\|f_{h}^{"}\|_{\infty}\mathbb{E}\big[|T|\big]

(1023)

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}[|T|].

(1024)

Finally, from (
1013
), (
1019
), and (
1024
), we have

\displaystyle\tilde{d}_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{f_{h}:h\in\mathcal{H}}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|T\big(f_{h}(T)-f_{h}(0)-Tf_{h}^{\prime}(0)\big)\big|\big]+\mathbb{E}[\big|f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(0)-f_{h}^{{}^{\prime}}(T)\big|]

(1025)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|^{3}\bigg\}\bigg]+2\mathbb{E}\big[|T|\big]+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}|T^{2}-1|.

(1026)

Now, observe that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|^{3}\bigg\}\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|^{3}\bigg\}\bigg||T|\leq 1\bigg]\mathbb{P}[|T|\leq 1]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|^{3}\bigg\}\big||T|>1\bigg]\mathbb{P}[|T|>1]

(1027)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|\bigg\}\bigg||T|\leq 1\bigg]\mathbb{P}[|T|\leq 1]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|\bigg\}\big||T|>1\bigg]\mathbb{P}[|T|>1]

(1028)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\big|T\big|\bigg||T|\leq 1\bigg]\mathbb{P}[|T|\leq 1]

\displaystyle\qquad+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|^{3}\bigg\}\big||T|>1\bigg]\mathbb{P}[|T|>1]

(1029)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|T|\big]+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\min\bigg\{\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi},\big|T\big|^{3}\bigg\}\big||T|>1\bigg]\mathbb{P}[|T|>1]

(1030)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|T|\big]+\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi}\mathbb{P}[|T|>1]

(1031)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\mathbb{E}\big[|T|\big]+\bigg(8+\frac{2}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi}\mathbb{E}[|T|]

(1032)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\bigg(10+\frac{1}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi}\mathbb{E}[|T|],

(1033)

where (
1028
) follows from
|T|^{3}\leq|T|
for all
|T|\leq 1
, (
1030
) follows from
\mathbb{E}[X]=\mathbb{E}[X|A]\mathbb{P}(A)+\mathbb{E}[X|A^{c}]\mathbb{P}(A^{c})\geq\mathbb{E}[X|A]\mathbb{P}(A)
for all non-negative random variable
X
, and (
1032
) follows from Markov’s inequality.

From (
1026
) it follows that

\displaystyle\tilde{d}_{W,\rm{mod}}(T,Z)

\displaystyle\leq\bigg(\bigg(10+\frac{1}{\pi}\bigg)\sqrt{2\pi}+\bigg(4+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\bigg)\bigg)\mathbb{E}[|T|]+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\mathbb{E}\big[|T^{2}-1|\big]

(1034)

\displaystyle<40\mathbb{E}[|T|]+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\mathbb{E}\big[|T^{2}-1|\big].

(1035)

By combining Lemma
17
and (
1035
), we have

\displaystyle d_{K}(T,Z)

\displaystyle<2(8\pi)^{-1/4}\sqrt{40\mathbb{E}[|T|]+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\mathbb{E}\big[|T^{2}-1|\big]}

(1036)

\displaystyle\leq 14(8\pi)^{-1/4}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}[|T|]+\mathbb{E}\big[|T^{2}-1|\big]}.

(1037)

This concludes our last proof.
